// ============================================================
// CALCULATOR.JS вЂ” Р›РћР“РРљРђ РљРђР›Р¬РљРЈР›РЇРўРћР Рђ
// Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ РїРѕ РєРЅРѕРїРєРµ В«РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРё!В» (РёР»Рё Enter РІ РїРѕР»Рµ СЃСѓРјРјС‹).
// ============================================================

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
    const K = window.KOPI || {};
    const isAuth = !!K.isAuth;

    const costInput = document.getElementById('cost');
    const frequencySelect = document.getElementById('frequency');
    const calcBtn = document.getElementById('calcBtn');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetBtn');
    const resultAmount = document.getElementById('resultAmount');
    const piggyFill = document.getElementById('piggyFill');
    const piggyEmoji = document.getElementById('piggyEmoji');
    const piggyMessage = document.getElementById('piggyMessage');
    const dreamPercent = document.getElementById('dreamPercent');
    const dreamName = document.getElementById('dreamName');
    const dreamPrice = document.getElementById('dreamPrice');
    const setDreamBtn = document.getElementById('setDreamBtn');
    const year2 = document.getElementById('year2');
    const year5 = document.getElementById('year5');
    const year10 = document.getElementById('year10');
    const inflationValue = document.getElementById('inflationValue');

    // РЎС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° СЃ РєР°Р»СЊРєСѓР»СЏС‚РѕСЂРѕРј? (РЅР° РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹С… СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°С… СЌС‚РѕС‚ С„Р°Р№Р» С‚РѕР¶Рµ РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅ)
    const onCalculatorPage = !!(costInput && frequencySelect && calcBtn && resultAmount);

    let chartInstance = null;
    let lastYearly = 0;
    let busy = false;

    // ===== РћР‘Р©РР• Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РР (РЅСѓР¶РЅС‹ С„СѓС‚РµСЂСѓ РЅР° РІСЃРµС… СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°С…) =====
    window.shareResult = function () {
        const el = document.getElementById('resultAmount');
        const total = el ? el.textContent.trim() : '';
        const text = total && total !== '0 в‚Ѕ'
            ? `рџђ· РЇ РјРѕРіСѓ РЅР°РєРѕРїРёС‚СЊ ${total} Р·Р° РіРѕРґ СЃ РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРёР»РєРѕР№! РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№ Рё С‚С‹! рџ’°`
            : 'рџђ· РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРёР»РєР° вЂ” РїРѕСЃС‡РёС‚Р°Р№, СЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РЅР°РєРѕРїРёС‚СЊ Р·Р° РіРѕРґ! рџ’°';

        if (navigator.share) {
            navigator.share({ title: 'РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРёР»РєР°', text: text, url: window.location.href }).catch(() => {});
        } else if (navigator.clipboard) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
                alert('рџ“‹ Р РµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚ СЃРєРѕРїРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ! РџРѕРґРµР»РёСЃСЊ СЃ РґСЂСѓР·СЊСЏРјРё! рџђ·');
            }).catch(() => { prompt('РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂСѓР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚:', text); });
        } else {
            prompt('РЎРєРѕРїРёСЂСѓР№ С‚РµРєСЃС‚:', text);
        }
    };

    window.resetAll = function () {
        if (!confirm('рџђ· РЎР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ РІСЃС‘?')) return;
        if (onCalculatorPage) {
            costInput.value = '0';
            frequencySelect.value = '260';
            resetView();
        } else {
            window.location.href = '/';
        }
    };

    window.exportPDF = function () {
        const element = document.getElementById('app');
        if (typeof html2pdf === 'undefined' || !element) {
            alert('Р­РєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚ PDF СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ.');
            return;
        }
        const originalOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
        html2pdf()
            .set({
                margin: [10, 10, 10, 10],
                filename: 'РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРёР»РєР°_РѕС‚С‡РµС‚.pdf',
                html2canvas: { scale: 2, useCORS: true, logging: false },
                jsPDF: { unit: 'mm', format: 'a4', orientation: 'portrait' }
            })
            .from(element)
            .save()
            .then(() => { document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow; })
            .catch((err) => {
                document.body.style.overflow = originalOverflow;
                console.error('PDF РѕС€РёР±РєР°:', err);
                alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЌРєСЃРїРѕСЂС‚Р°. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№ РїРѕР·Р¶Рµ.');
            });
    };

    if (!onCalculatorPage) return;

    // ===== РЎР‘Р РћРЎ РћРўРћР‘Р РђР–Р•РќРРЇ =====
    function resetView() {
        lastYearly = 0;
        resultAmount.innerHTML = '0 <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
        piggyFill.style.width = '0%';
        piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
        piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџђ· Р’РІРµРґРё СЃСѓРјРјСѓ Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРё В«РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРё!В»';
        year2.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
        year5.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
        year10.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
        inflationValue.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
        updateDream(0);
        updateChart(Array(12).fill(0));
    }

    // ===== Р РђРЎР§РЃРў (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕ РЅР°Р¶Р°С‚РёСЋ РєРЅРѕРїРєРё) =====
    function calculate() {
        if (busy) return;

        const cost = parseFloat(costInput.value) || 0;
        const days = parseInt(frequencySelect.value) || 0;

        if (cost <= 0) {
            resetView();
            return;
        }

        busy = true;
        fetch(K.routes.calculate, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'Accept': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': K.csrf
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ cost, frequency: days })
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (typeof data.yearly === 'undefined') {
                throw new Error('РќРµРєРѕСЂСЂРµРєС‚РЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂР°');
            }

            lastYearly = data.yearly;
            animateNumber(resultAmount, data.yearly);
            piggyFill.style.width = data.percent + '%';

            const remaining = data.target - data.yearly;

            if (data.percent >= 100) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ·';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџЋ‰ РЈСЂР°! Р¦РµР»СЊ РґРѕСЃС‚РёРіРЅСѓС‚Р°! РўС‹ РјРѕР»РѕРґРµС†! рџЋ‰';
                triggerCelebration();
            } else if (data.percent >= 70) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђЅ';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџ”Ґ РџРѕС‡С‚Рё Сѓ С†РµР»Рё! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + remaining.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ!';
            } else if (data.percent >= 30) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџ’Є РҐРѕСЂРѕС€РёР№ С‚РµРјРї! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + remaining.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ РґРѕ С†РµР»Рё.';
            } else if (data.percent > 0) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџђ· РќР°С‡РёРЅР°Р№ РєРѕРїРёС‚СЊ! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + remaining.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ РґРѕ С†РµР»Рё.';
            } else {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџђ· РќР°С‡РёРЅР°Р№ РєРѕРїРёС‚СЊ СѓР¶Рµ СЃРµРіРѕРґРЅСЏ!';
            }

            piggyEmoji.style.transform = 'scale(1.2) rotate(-5deg)';
            setTimeout(() => { piggyEmoji.style.transform = 'scale(1) rotate(0deg)'; }, 400);

            year2.textContent = data.two_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            year5.textContent = data.five_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            year10.textContent = data.ten_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            inflationValue.textContent = data.real_value.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            updateDream(data.yearly);
            updateChart(data.monthly);
        })
        .catch(error => {
            console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚Р°:', error);
            alert('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊ СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚. РћР±РЅРѕРІРё СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ Рё РїРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·.');
        })
        .finally(() => { busy = false; });
    }

    // ===== РђРќРРњРђР¦РРЇ Р§РРЎР›Рђ =====
    function animateNumber(element, target, duration = 800) {
        const start = parseFloat(element.textContent.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0;
        const diff = target - start;
        if (diff === 0 || target === 0) {
            element.innerHTML = target.toLocaleString() + ' <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
            return;
        }
        const steps = 40;
        const step = diff / steps;
        let current = start;
        let count = 0;
        const timer = setInterval(() => {
            count++;
            current += step;
            if (count >= steps) {
                current = target;
                clearInterval(timer);
            }
            element.innerHTML = Math.round(current).toLocaleString() + ' <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
        }, duration / steps);
    }

    // ===== РњР•Р§РўРђ =====
    function updateDream(yearly) {
        if (!dreamPercent || !dreamPrice) return;
        const price = parseFloat(dreamPrice.value);
        if (isNaN(price) || price <= 0) {
            dreamPercent.textContent = '0%';
            return;
        }
        const percent = Math.min((yearly / price) * 100, 100);
        dreamPercent.textContent = percent >= 100 ? 'рџЋ‰ Р”РѕСЃС‚РёРіРЅСѓС‚Р°! рџЋ‰' : percent.toFixed(1) + '%';
    }

    // ===== Р—РђР“Р РЈР—РљРђ РњР•Р§РўР« (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ РІРѕС€РµРґС€РёС… РїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°С‚РµР»РµР№) =====
    function loadDream() {
        if (!isAuth || !K.routes.dreamGet || !dreamName || !dreamPrice) return;
        fetch(K.routes.dreamGet, { headers: { 'Accept': 'application/json' } })
            .then(response => response.ok ? response.json() : null)
            .then(data => {
                if (data && data.has_dream) {
                    dreamName.value = data.dream_name;
                    dreamPrice.value = parseFloat(data.dream_price);
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {});
    }

    // ===== Р“Р РђР¤РРљ =====
    function chartColors() {
        const isDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
        return {
            text: isDark ? '#fed7aa' : '#5c4033',
            grid: isDark ? '#4d3728' : '#fde4c8'
        };
    }

    function updateChart(monthlyData) {
        const ctx = document.getElementById('savingsChart');
        if (!ctx || typeof Chart === 'undefined') return;

        const c = chartColors();
        if (chartInstance) chartInstance.destroy();

        chartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: ['РЇРЅРІ', 'Р¤РµРІ', 'РњР°СЂ', 'РђРїСЂ', 'РњР°Р№', 'РСЋРЅ', 'РСЋР»', 'РђРІРі', 'РЎРµРЅ', 'РћРєС‚', 'РќРѕСЏ', 'Р”РµРє'],
                datasets: [{
                    label: 'РќР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРёСЏ',
                    data: monthlyData || Array(12).fill(0),
                    borderColor: '#fb923c',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.12)',
                    fill: true,
                    tension: 0.3,
                    pointBackgroundColor: '#fb923c',
                    pointRadius: 5,
                    borderWidth: 3
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                maintainAspectRatio: true,
                plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                scales: {
                    x: { grid: { color: c.grid }, ticks: { color: c.text, font: { size: 11 } } },
                    y: {
                        grid: { color: c.grid },
                        ticks: {
                            color: c.text,
                            font: { size: 11 },
                            callback: function (value) { return value.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ'; }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    window.updateChartTheme = function () {
        if (!chartInstance) return;
        const c = chartColors();
        chartInstance.options.scales.x.ticks.color = c.text;
        chartInstance.options.scales.x.grid.color = c.grid;
        chartInstance.options.scales.y.ticks.color = c.text;
        chartInstance.options.scales.y.grid.color = c.grid;
        chartInstance.update();
    };

    // ===== РљРћРќР¤Р•РўРўР =====
    function triggerCelebration() {
        const emojis = ['рџЋ‰', 'вњЁ', 'рџЊџ', 'рџЋЉ', 'рџ’«', 'рџђ·', 'рџ’°'];
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            const el = document.createElement('span');
            el.textContent = emojis[Math.floor(Math.random() * emojis.length)];
            el.style.cssText = `
                position: fixed;
                left: ${Math.random() * 100}%;
                top: -20px;
                font-size: ${20 + Math.random() * 30}px;
                pointer-events: none;
                z-index: 9999;
                transition: all ${1 + Math.random() * 2}s ease-in;
                opacity: 1;
            `;
            document.body.appendChild(el);

            setTimeout(() => {
                el.style.top = (80 + Math.random() * 20) + '%';
                el.style.left = (Math.random() * 100) + '%';
                el.style.opacity = '0';
                el.style.transform = 'rotate(' + (Math.random() * 720) + 'deg)';
            }, 50);

            setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 3500);
        }
    }

    // ===== РЎРћРҐР РђРќР•РќРР• РњР•Р§РўР« =====
    if (setDreamBtn) {
        setDreamBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            if (!isAuth) {
                alert('рџђ· Р§С‚РѕР±С‹ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ РјРµС‡С‚Сѓ, РІРѕР№РґРё РІ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚.');
                window.location.href = '/login';
                return;
            }

            const name = dreamName.value.trim();
            const price = parseFloat(dreamPrice.value);

            if (!name || !price || price <= 0) {
                alert('рџђ· Р’РІРµРґРё РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ Рё СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРІРѕРµР№ РјРµС‡С‚С‹!');
                return;
            }

            fetch(K.routes.dreamUpdate, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': K.csrf
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({ dream_name: name, dream_price: price })
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    alert('рџЋЇ РњРµС‡С‚Р° "' + data.dream_name + '" СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР°! РљРѕРїРё РґР°Р»СЊС€Рµ! рџђ·');
                    updateDream(lastYearly); // Р±РµР· РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚Р° Рё Р±РµР· Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё РІ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РјРµС‡С‚С‹:', error);
            });
        });
    }

    // ===== РљРќРћРџРљР (Р°РІС‚РѕСЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚Р° РїСЂРё РІРІРѕРґРµ Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РЅРµС‚) =====
    calcBtn.addEventListener('click', calculate);

    costInput.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            calculate();
        }
    });

    if (resetBtn) {
        resetBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            costInput.value = '0';
            frequencySelect.value = '260';
            resetView();
        });
    }

    // ===== РРќРР¦РРђР›РР—РђР¦РРЇ (Р±РµР· СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚Р°) =====
    updateChart(Array(12).fill(0));
    loadDream();
});

<?php

use Illuminate\Contracts\Http\Kernel;
use Illuminate\Http\Request;

define('LARAVEL_START', microtime(true));

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Check If The Application Is Under Maintenance
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| If the application is in maintenance / demo mode via the "down" command
| we will load this file so that any pre-rendered content can be shown
| instead of starting the framework, which could cause an exception.
|
*/

if (file_exists($maintenance = __DIR__.'/../storage/framework/maintenance.php')) {
    require $maintenance;
}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Register The Auto Loader
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Composer provides a convenient, automatically generated class loader for
| this application. We just need to utilize it! We'll simply require it
| into the script here so we don't need to manually load our classes.
|
*/

require __DIR__.'/../vendor/autoload.php';

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Run The Application
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Once we have the application, we can handle the incoming request using
| the application's HTTP kernel. Then, we will send the response back
| to this client's browser, allowing them to enjoy our application.
|
*/

$app = require_once __DIR__.'/../bootstrap/app.php';

$kernel = $app->make(Kernel::class);

$response = $kernel->handle(
    $request = Request::capture()
)->send();

$kernel->terminate($request, $response);

=== ФАЙЛ: C:\OSPanel\domains\copicop1\resources\js\app.js ===

// ===== РўР•РњРђ =====
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const themeToggle = document.getElementById('themeToggle');
    if (!themeToggle) return;

    // Р¤СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ РїСЂРёРјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ С‚РµРјС‹
    function applyTheme(theme) {
        const html = document.documentElement;
        if (theme === 'dark') {
            html.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');
            if (themeToggle) themeToggle.textContent = 'вЂпёЏ РЎРІРµС‚Р»Р°СЏ';
            document.cookie = 'theme=dark; path=/; max-age=31536000';
        } else {
            html.removeAttribute('data-theme');
            if (themeToggle) themeToggle.textContent = 'рџЊ™ РўС‘РјРЅР°СЏ';
            document.cookie = 'theme=light; path=/; max-age=31536000';
        }
        
        // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РіСЂР°С„РёРє, РµСЃР»Рё РѕРЅ РµСЃС‚СЊ
        if (window.updateChartTheme) {
            window.updateChartTheme();
        }
    }

    // Р—Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅРЅРѕР№ С‚РµРјС‹
    function loadTheme() {
        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј cookie
        const cookies = document.cookie.split(';');
        let savedTheme = null;
        for (let cookie of cookies) {
            const [name, value] = cookie.trim().split('=');
            if (name === 'theme') {
                savedTheme = value;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        // Р•СЃР»Рё С‚РµРјР° СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅР° РІ cookie вЂ” РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј
        if (savedTheme) {
            applyTheme(savedTheme);
        } else {
            // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј localStorage (РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё)
            const localTheme = localStorage.getItem('kopi-theme');
            if (localTheme) {
                applyTheme(localTheme);
            } else {
                // РџРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ вЂ” СЃРІРµС‚Р»Р°СЏ
                applyTheme('light');
            }
        }
    }

    // РџСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј С‚РµРјСѓ РїСЂРё Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РєРµ
    loadTheme();

    // РћР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚С‡РёРє РєР»РёРєР° РїРѕ РєРЅРѕРїРєРµ
    if (themeToggle) {
        themeToggle.addEventListener('click', function() {
            const html = document.documentElement;
            const isDark = html.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
            applyTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
            
            // РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РІ localStorage РґР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕР№ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё
            localStorage.setItem('kopi-theme', isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
        });
    }

    // ===== FLASH РЎРћРћР‘Р©Р•РќРРЇ =====
    const flash = document.getElementById('flashMessage');
    if (flash) {
        setTimeout(() => {
            flash.style.opacity = '0';
            flash.style.transform = 'translateX(100%)';
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (flash.parentNode) {
                    flash.remove();
                }
            }, 500);
        }, 4000);
    }

    console.log('рџЊ“ РўС‘РјРЅР°СЏ С‚РµРјР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅР°!');
});

=== ФАЙЛ: C:\OSPanel\domains\copicop1\resources\js\calculator.js ===

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const costInput = document.getElementById('cost');
    const frequencySelect = document.getElementById('frequency');
    const calcBtn = document.getElementById('calcBtn');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('resetBtn');
    const resultAmount = document.getElementById('resultAmount');
    const piggyFill = document.getElementById('piggyFill');
    const piggyEmoji = document.getElementById('piggyEmoji');
    const piggyMessage = document.getElementById('piggyMessage');
    const dreamPercent = document.getElementById('dreamPercent');
    const dreamName = document.getElementById('dreamName');
    const dreamPrice = document.getElementById('dreamPrice');
    const setDreamBtn = document.getElementById('setDreamBtn');
    const year2 = document.getElementById('year2');
    const year5 = document.getElementById('year5');
    const year10 = document.getElementById('year10');
    const inflationValue = document.getElementById('inflationValue');

    let chartInstance = null;

    // ===== Р РђРЎР§РЃРў =====
    function calculate() {
        const cost = parseFloat(costInput.value) || 0;
        const days = parseInt(frequencySelect.value) || 0;

        // Р•СЃР»Рё cost = 0, РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµРј 0
        if (cost <= 0) {
            resultAmount.innerHTML = '0 <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
            piggyFill.style.width = '0%';
            piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
            piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџђ· Р’РІРµРґРё СЃСѓРјРјСѓ Рё РЅР°Р¶РјРё "РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРё!"';
            year2.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
            year5.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
            year10.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
            inflationValue.textContent = '0 в‚Ѕ';
            updateDream(0);
            return;
        }

        fetch(window.KOPI.routes.calculate, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': window.KOPI.csrf
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ cost, frequency: days })
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {
            // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚
            animateNumber(resultAmount, data.yearly);

            // РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РєРѕРїРёР»РєСѓ
            piggyFill.style.width = data.percent + '%';

            // РЎРІРёРЅСЊСЏ РјРµРЅСЏРµС‚ СЌРјРѕС†РёРё
            if (data.percent >= 100) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ·';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџЋ‰ РЈСЂР°! Р¦РµР»СЊ РґРѕСЃС‚РёРіРЅСѓС‚Р°! РўС‹ РјРѕР»РѕРґРµС†! рџЋ‰';
                triggerCelebration();
            } else if (data.percent >= 70) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђЅ';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџ”Ґ РџРѕС‡С‚Рё Сѓ С†РµР»Рё! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + (data.target - data.yearly).toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ!';
            } else if (data.percent >= 30) {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџ’Є РҐРѕСЂРѕС€РёР№ С‚РµРјРї! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + (data.target - data.yearly).toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ РґРѕ С†РµР»Рё.';
            } else {
                piggyEmoji.textContent = 'рџђ–';
                piggyMessage.textContent = 'рџђ· РќР°С‡РёРЅР°Р№ РєРѕРїРёС‚СЊ! РћСЃС‚Р°Р»РѕСЃСЊ ' + (data.target - data.yearly).toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ РґРѕ С†РµР»Рё.';
            }

            piggyEmoji.style.transform = 'scale(1.2) rotate(-5deg)';
            setTimeout(() => {
                piggyEmoji.style.transform = 'scale(1) rotate(0deg)';
            }, 400);

            // Р”РѕР»РіРѕСЃСЂРѕС‡РЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРѕРіРЅРѕР·
            year2.textContent = data.two_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            year5.textContent = data.five_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
            year10.textContent = data.ten_years.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';

            // РРЅС„Р»СЏС†РёСЏ
            inflationValue.textContent = data.real_value.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';

            // РњРµС‡С‚Р°
            updateDream(data.yearly);

            // Р“СЂР°С„РёРє
            updateChart(data.monthly);
        });
    }

    // ===== РђРќРРњРђР¦РРЇ Р§РРЎР›Рђ =====
    function animateNumber(element, target, duration = 800) {
        const start = parseFloat(element.textContent.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0;
        const diff = target - start;
        if (diff === 0 || target === 0) {
            element.innerHTML = target.toLocaleString() + ' <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
            return;
        }
        const steps = 40;
        const step = diff / steps;
        let current = start;
        let count = 0;
        const timer = setInterval(() => {
            count++;
            current += step;
            if (count >= steps) {
                current = target;
                clearInterval(timer);
            }
            element.innerHTML = Math.round(current).toLocaleString() + ' <span>в‚Ѕ</span>';
        }, duration / steps);
    }

    // ===== РњР•Р§РўРђ =====
    function updateDream(yearly) {
        const price = parseFloat(dreamPrice.value);
        if (isNaN(price) || price <= 0) {
            dreamPercent.textContent = '0%';
            return;
        }
        const percent = Math.min((yearly / price) * 100, 100);
        dreamPercent.textContent = percent.toFixed(1) + '%';
        if (percent >= 100) {
            dreamPercent.textContent = 'рџЋ‰ Р”РѕСЃС‚РёРіРЅСѓС‚Р°! рџЋ‰';
        }
    }

    // ===== Р—РђР“Р РЈР—РљРђ РњР•Р§РўР« РР— РџР РћР¤РР›РЇ =====
    function loadDream() {
        fetch(window.KOPI.routes.dreamGet)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.has_dream) {
                    dreamName.value = data.dream_name;
                    dreamPrice.value = data.dream_price;
                    // РџРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµРј СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј РјРµС‡С‚С‹
                    setTimeout(calculate, 100);
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {
                // Р•СЃР»Рё РѕС€РёР±РєР° вЂ” РёРіРЅРѕСЂРёСЂСѓРµРј
            });
    }

    // ===== Р“Р РђР¤РРљ =====
    function updateChart(monthlyData) {
        const ctx = document.getElementById('savingsChart');
        if (!ctx) return;

        const isDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
        const textColor = isDark ? '#cbd5e1' : '#5c4033';
        const gridColor = isDark ? '#2d2d4a' : '#fde4c8';

        if (chartInstance) {
            chartInstance.destroy();
        }

        chartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: ['РЇРЅРІ', 'Р¤РµРІ', 'РњР°СЂ', 'РђРїСЂ', 'РњР°Р№', 'РСЋРЅ', 'РСЋР»', 'РђРІРі', 'РЎРµРЅ', 'РћРєС‚', 'РќРѕСЏ', 'Р”РµРє'],
                datasets: [{
                    label: 'РќР°РєРѕРїР»РµРЅРёСЏ',
                    data: monthlyData,
                    borderColor: '#fb923c',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(251, 146, 60, 0.12)',
                    fill: true,
                    tension: 0.3,
                    pointBackgroundColor: '#fb923c',
                    pointRadius: 5,
                    borderWidth: 3
                }]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                maintainAspectRatio: true,
                plugins: {
                    legend: { display: false }
                },
                scales: {
                    x: {
                        grid: { color: gridColor },
                        ticks: { color: textColor, font: { size: 11, family: 'Comfortaa' } }
                    },
                    y: {
                        grid: { color: gridColor },
                        ticks: {
                            color: textColor,
                            font: { size: 11, family: 'Comfortaa' },
                            callback: function(value) {
                                return value.toLocaleString() + ' в‚Ѕ';
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // ===== РљРћРќР¤Р•РўРўР =====
    function triggerCelebration() {
        const emojis = ['рџЋ‰', 'вњЁ', 'рџЊџ', 'рџЋЉ', 'рџ’«', 'рџђ·', 'рџЄ™'];
        for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
            const el = document.createElement('span');
            el.textContent = emojis[Math.floor(Math.random() * emojis.length)];
            el.style.cssText = `
                position: fixed;

        }

    setDreamBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {

            alert('рџђ· Р’РІРµРґРё РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ Рё СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРІРѕРµР№ РјРµС‡С‚С‹!');
            return;

        fetch(window.KOPI.routes.dreamUpdate, {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': window.KOPI.csrf
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
                dream_name: dreamName.value.trim(),
                dream_price: parseFloat(dreamPrice.value)
            })
        })
        .then(response => response.json())
        .then(data => {

                calculate();
            }
        });
    });

window.updateChartTheme = function() {
    if (chartInstance) {
        const isDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark';
        const textColor = isDark ? '#cbd5e1' : '#5c4033';
        const gridColor = isDark ? '#2d2d4a' : '#fde4c8';
        
        chartInstance.options.scales.x.ticks.color = textColor;
        chartInstance.options.scales.x.grid.color = gridColor;
        chartInstance.options.scales.y.ticks.color = textColor;
        chartInstance.options.scales.y.grid.color = gridColor;
        chartInstance.update();
    }
};
    console.log('рџђ· РљРѕРїРё-РљРѕРїРёР»РєР° Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР¶РµРЅР°! РљРѕРїРё СЃ СЂР°РґРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ! рџ’°');
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    const bg = document.getElementById('coinsBg');
    if (!bg) return;

    const emojis = ['рџЄ™', 'рџ’°', 'вњЁ', 'рџђ·', 'рџЊџ', 'рџ’Ћ'];
    
    for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
        const el = document.createElement('span');
        el.className = 'coin-particle';
        el.textContent = emojis[Math.floor(Math.random() * emojis.length)];
        el.style.left = Math.random() * 100 + '%';
        el.style.fontSize = (20 + Math.random() * 30) + 'px';
        el.style.animationDuration = (15 + Math.random() * 25) + 's';
        el.style.animationDelay = (Math.random() * 20) + 's';
        bg.appendChild(el);
    }
});

@push('scripts')
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-save-tip').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                const id = this.dataset.id;
                fetch(`/tips/${id}/save`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '{{ csrf_token() }}'
                    }
                })
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.success) {
                        if (data.saved) {
                            this.textContent = 'в­ђ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ';
                            this.classList.add('saved');
                        } else {
                            this.textContent = 'рџ’ѕ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ';
                            this.classList.remove('saved');
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    });
</script>
@endpush

<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        // Р”РѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РєРё
        document.getElementById('addHabitBtn').addEventListener('click', function() {
            const name = document.getElementById('habitName').value.trim();
            const cost = parseFloat(document.getElementById('habitCost').value);
            const frequency = parseInt(document.getElementById('habitFreq').value);

            if (!name || isNaN(cost) || cost <= 0) {
                alert('рџђ· Р’РІРµРґРё РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ Рё С†РµРЅСѓ РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РєРё!');
                return;
            }

            fetch('{{ route("habits.store") }}', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'Accept': 'application/json',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '{{ csrf_token() }}'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({ name, cost, frequency })
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.success) {
                    location.reload();
                }
            });
        });

        const delHeaders = {
            'Accept': 'application/json',
            'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '{{ csrf_token() }}'
        };

        function sendDelete(url) {
            return fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE', headers: delHeaders })
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.success) { location.reload(); }
                    else { alert('РќРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ СѓРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·.'); }
                })
                .catch(() => alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёСЏ. РџРѕРїСЂРѕР±СѓР№ РµС‰С‘ СЂР°Р·.'));
        }

        // РЈРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РѕРґРЅРѕР№ РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РєРё
        document.querySelectorAll('.delete-habit').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РєСѓ?')) return;
                sendDelete(`/habits/${this.dataset.id}`);
            });
        });

        // РћС‡РёСЃС‚РєР° РІСЃРµС… РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РµРє
        const clearBtn = document.getElementById('clearHabitsBtn');
        if (clearBtn) {
            clearBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ Р’РЎР• РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РєРё?')) return;
                sendDelete('{{ route("habits.clear") }}');
            });
        }
    });
</script>

                    {{ in_array($tip->id, $savedTipIds) ? 'в­ђ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ' : 'рџ’ѕ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ' }}
                </button>

        @endforeach

    <div class="inflation-box mt-20" style="border-color: #b8873b;">
        <div class="label">рџЊџ Р“Р»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚ РєРѕРїРёР»РєРё</div>
        <div class="value" style="font-size:18px; font-weight:700; color:var(--text-primary);">
            "РќР°С‡РЅРё СЃ 2-3 РїСЂРёРІС‹С‡РµРє, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ Р»РµРіС‡Рµ РІСЃРµРіРѕ Р·Р°РјРµРЅРёС‚СЊ, Рё РєРѕРїРё СЃ СЂР°РґРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ!" рџђ·

@push('scripts')
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        document.querySelectorAll('.btn-save-tip').forEach(btn => {
            btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
                const id = this.dataset.id;
                fetch(`/tips/${id}/save`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'X-CSRF-TOKEN': '{{ csrf_token() }}'
                    }
                })
                .then(response => response.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.success) {
                        if (data.saved) {
                            this.textContent = 'в­ђ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ';
                            this.classList.add('saved');
                        } else {
                            this.textContent = 'рџ’ѕ РЎРѕС…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЊ';
                            this.classList.remove('saved');
                        }
                    }
                });
            });
        });
    });
</script>
@endpush

=== ФАЙЛ: C:\OSPanel\domains\copicop1\routes\channels.php ===

<?php

use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Broadcast;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Broadcast Channels
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| Here you may register all of the event broadcasting channels that your
| application supports. The given channel authorization callbacks are
| used to check if an authenticated user can listen to the channel.
|
*/

Broadcast::channel('App.Models.User.{id}', function ($user, $id) {
    return (int) $user->id === (int) $id;
});

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Console Routes
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
|
| This file is where you may define all of your Closure based console
| commands. Each Closure is bound to a command instance allowing a
| simple approach to interacting with each command's IO methods.
|
*/

Artisan::command('inspire', function () {
    $this->comment(Inspiring::quote());
})->purpose('Display an inspiring quote');

// ===== Р“РћРЎРўР•Р’Р«Р• РњРђР РЁР РЈРўР« =====

    Route::post('/login', [LoginController::class, 'login']);
    Route::get('/register', [RegisterController::class, 'showRegistrationForm'])->name('register');
    Route::post('/register', [RegisterController::class, 'register']);
});

// ===== Р’Р«РҐРћР” =====
Route::post('/logout', [LoginController::class, 'logout'])->name('logout')->middleware('auth');

// ===== РЎРћРҐР РђРќР•РќРР• РўР•РњР« =====
Route::post('/theme', function (Request $request) {
    $theme = $request->input('theme');
    if (in_array($theme, ['light', 'dark'])) {
        session(['theme' => $theme]);
        return response()->json(['success' => true]);
    }
    return response()->json(['success' => false], 400);
})->name('theme.save')->middleware('auth');

// ===== Р Р•Р”РР Р•РљРў РЎРўРђР РћР™ Р“Р›РђР’РќРћР™ =====

})->name('dashboard');

    Route::delete('/history/{id}', [HistoryController::class, 'destroy'])->name('history.destroy');

    Route::get('/habits/{id}/edit', [HabitController::class, 'edit'])->name('habits.edit');
    Route::put('/habits/{id}', [HabitController::class, 'update'])->name('habits.update');
    Route::delete('/habits', [HabitController::class, 'clear'])->name('habits.clear');
    Route::delete('/habits/{id}', [HabitController::class, 'destroy'])->name('habits.destroy');

=== ФАЙЛ: C:\OSPanel\domains\copicop1\tests\CreatesApplication.php ===

<?php

namespace Tests;

use Illuminate\Contracts\Console\Kernel;
use Illuminate\Foundation\Application;

trait CreatesApplication
{
    /**
     * Creates the application.
     */
    public function createApplication(): Application
    {
        $app = require __DIR__.'/../bootstrap/app.php';

        $app->make(Kernel::class)->bootstrap();

        return $app;
    }
}
